# transaction.preflight

Generated from the CLI request and result definitions. Do not edit this file.

Validate an atomic plan with actual domain preparation, without committing it.

Tier: `advanced`. The result is `{data, meta}`; see [common envelopes and types](common.md).

| Input | Presence | Type and constraints |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `actor_id` | required | string; max UTF-8 bytes=96; Validated coordination identity. |
| `at_revision` | optional | string; pattern=&quot;^(0&#124;[1-9][0-9]*)(?![\\s\\S])&quot;; maximum decimal=&quot;4611686018427387903&quot; |
| `operations` | required | array; max items=128; 1..128 ordered operations with unique creation aliases. |
| `run_id` | optional | string; max UTF-8 bytes=96; Validated coordination identity. |
| `workspace_id` | required | string; max UTF-8 bytes=96; Workspace identity. |

For preconditions and a small example, run `workgraph help transaction.preflight`. Use `--full` for the complete contracts below.

**Effect:** read. Reads recorded state without changing it.
